# Cyberpsycho Sighting: Bloody Ritual

Journal: `quests/minor_quest/ma_wat_nid_15` · `CyberPsycho` · hash `2290065945`.

Journal premise: Not everyone's made for Maelstrom. Some – surprise, surprise – don't take to well to having half their face chopped off. They start hearing voices, seeing and imagining things. Then, it's enough to get their hands on a gun, and... that recipe for disaster's ready to serve.

**Coverage: journal-linked.** 32 exact journal-linked nodes in 3 resources; 6 resources in the inspected dependency context.

Resource roles in that context: 5 content candidate, 1 repair. Role labels describe path/name evidence, not proof of runtime reachability.

Root quest resources with additional journal references: none indexed. Roots remain activation context when a phase/scene anchor exists, to avoid importing sibling launch scaffolding.

Generated structural analysis from decoded resources. Shared resources are context, not exclusive quest ownership. ## Required acceptance cases before reuse

Exercise normal completion and each named alternate outcome; interrupt and return at every scene handoff; start with spatial/item/actor predicates already satisfied; save/load across waits and joins; cancel parallel listeners; verify reward delivery once; and verify cleanup without changing the committed outcome. Apply the block-specific cases in the [expanded contracts](../all-quest-blocks.md). No installed playtest is claimed.

Events in different sections are not treated as one chronological timeline: parent routing, interruption and playback policy still apply. Animation/voice payloads and installed timing are not validated by this extraction.
